var evaluateAdAnalyticsClientIdentity = (function(a,b,c=!1){let d=[];if(a.hostname&&d.push({name:"hostname",matched:b.hostname===a.hostname,actual:b.hostname,expected:a.hostname}),a.primaryLanguage){let c=(b.primaryLanguage||"").toLowerCase();d.push({name:"primary_language",matched:c===a.primaryLanguage.toLowerCase(),actual:c,expected:a.primaryLanguage})}if(!c&&a.chromeFullVersion){let c=null==b.chromeFullVersion?null:b.chromeFullVersion===a.chromeFullVersion;d.push({name:"chrome_full_version",matched:c,actual:null===c?"unknown":c?"exact":"other",expected:"exact"})}return d});
var readInitialDocumentPathname = (function(){try{let a=window.performance.getEntriesByType("navigation")[0];if(!a||"string"!=typeof a.name||!/^https?:\/\//i.test(a.name))return null;let b=new URL(a.name);if(!["http:","https:"].includes(b.protocol)||b.origin!==window.location.origin)return null;return b.pathname}catch{return null}});

  function truncate(value, maxLength) {
    value = String(value || '');
    return value.length > maxLength ? value.slice(0, maxLength) + '...' : value;
  }

  function hasCookie(cookieString, name) {
    var prefix = name + '=';
    var isAuthCookie = authCookieNames.indexOf(name) >= 0;
    return String(cookieString || '').split(';').some(function (cookie) {
      var entry = cookie.replace(/^\s+/, '');
      return entry.indexOf(prefix) === 0 && (!isAuthCookie || entry.length > prefix.length);
    });
  }

  function hasCookiePrefix(cookieString, prefix) {
    return String(cookieString || '').split(';').some(function (cookie) {
      return cookie.trim().indexOf(prefix) === 0;
    });
  }

  function bucketPath(value) {
    var pathname = value || '/';
    try {
      pathname = new URL(pathname, window.location.origin).pathname;
    } catch (error) {
      pathname = String(pathname).split('?')[0] || '/';
    }
    pathname = (pathname || '/').replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
    pathname = pathname.replace(/^\/(?:en|ko|ja|pl|fr|de|es|es-419|nl|da|sv|no|ru|hu|fi|tr|ro|pt|zh-cn|zh-tw|ar|sr|it|th|vi|cs|el|id)(?=\/|$)/i, '') || '/';

    var parts = pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
    if (parts[0] !== 'lol') {
      return parts[0] ? '/' + parts[0] : '/';
    }

    var section = parts[1] || 'root';
    if (section === 'summoners') {
      return '/lol/summoners/:region/:summoner';
    }
    if (section === 'champions') {
      return parts.length > 2 ? '/lol/champions/:champion' : '/lol/champions';
    }
    if (section === 'leaderboards') {
      return parts.length > 2 ? '/lol/leaderboards/' + parts[2] : '/lol/leaderboards';
    }
    if (section === 'modes') {
      return parts.length > 2 ? '/lol/modes/:mode' : '/lol/modes';
    }
    return '/lol/' + section;
  }

  function bucketReferrer(value) {
    if (!value) {
      return 'none';
    }
    try {
      var url = new URL(value);
      var hostname = url.hostname.toLowerCase();
      if (hostname === 'op.gg' || hostname.slice(-6) === '.op.gg') {
        return 'same_origin:' + bucketPath(url.pathname);
      }
      if (/(google|bing|naver|yahoo|duckduckgo|baidu|yandex)\./.test(hostname)) {
        return 'search';
      }
      if (/(facebook|instagram|twitter|x|reddit|discord|youtube|tiktok)\./.test(hostname)) {
        return 'social';
      }
      return 'external:' + truncate(hostname, 80);
    } catch (error) {
      return 'invalid';
    }
  }

  function getBrowserBucket(userAgent) {
    var matchers = [
      ['edge', /EdgA?\/(\d+)/],
      ['opera', /OPR\/(\d+)/],
      ['samsung', /SamsungBrowser\/(\d+)/],
      ['chrome', /(?:Chrome|CriOS)\/(\d+)/],
      ['firefox', /Firefox\/(\d+)/],
      ['safari', /Version\/(\d+).+Safari/],
    ];
    for (var i = 0; i < matchers.length; i += 1) {
      var match = String(userAgent || '').match(matchers[i][1]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return matchers[i][0] + '_' + match[1];
      }
    }
    return userAgent ? 'other' : 'empty';
  }

  function getOsBucket(userAgent) {
    userAgent = String(userAgent || '');
    if (/Windows NT 10\.0/.test(userAgent)) {
      return 'windows_10';
    }
    if (/iPhone|iPad|iPod/.test(userAgent)) {
      return 'ios';
    }
    if (/Macintosh|Mac OS X/.test(userAgent)) {
      return 'macos';
    }
    if (/Android/.test(userAgent)) {
      return 'android';
    }
    if (/Linux/.test(userAgent)) {
      return 'linux';
    }
    return userAgent ? 'other' : 'empty';
  }

  function inRange(value, min, max) {
    if (typeof min === 'number' && value < min) {
      return false;
    }
    if (typeof max === 'number' && value > max) {
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  function matchesPattern(value, pattern) {
    try {
      return new RegExp(pattern).test(value);
    } catch (error) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function matchesLanguagePrefixes(languages, prefixes) {
    if (!Array.isArray(prefixes) || prefixes.length === 0) {
      return true;
    }
    return languages.some(function (language) {
      var normalizedLanguage = String(language || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      return prefixes.some(function (prefix) {
        return normalizedLanguage.indexOf(prefix) === 0;
      });
    });
  }

  function getDecision(matched, alreadySuppressed) {
    if (matched === null) {
      return alreadySuppressed ? 'already_suppressed_not_observed' : 'not_observed';
    }
    if (alreadySuppressed) {
      return matched ? 'already_suppressed_matched' : 'already_suppressed_not_matched';
    }
    return matched ? 'matched' : 'not_matched';
  }

  function evaluateRule(rule) {
    var conditions = rule && rule.clientConditions;
    var checks = [];
    var cookieString = document.cookie || '';
    var userAgent = (window.navigator && window.navigator.userAgent) || '';
    var languages = [];
    if (Array.isArray(window.navigator && window.navigator.languages)) {
      languages = languages.concat(window.navigator.languages);
    }
    if (window.navigator && window.navigator.language) {
      languages.push(window.navigator.language);
    }

    function addCheck(name, matched, actual, expected) {
      checks.push({ name: name, matched: Boolean(matched), actual: actual, expected: expected });
    }

    if (!conditions) {
      return { matched: false, conditionChecks: checks };
    }

    addCheck('supported_conditions', Object.keys(conditions).every(function (key) { return ["absentCookieNames","chromeFullVersion","devicePixelRatio","hostname","initialPathPrefixes","nonUserAgentPattern","pathPrefixes","primaryLanguage","referrer","requiredLanguagePrefixes","screen","userAgentPattern","viewport"].indexOf(key) >= 0; }), 'condition keys', 'supported');
    var identityHints = conditions.chromeFullVersion && window["__OPGG_AD_ANALYTICS_CHROME_HINTS__"];
    checks = checks.concat(evaluateAdAnalyticsClientIdentity(conditions, {
      hostname: conditions.hostname ? window.location.hostname : undefined,
      primaryLanguage: conditions.primaryLanguage ? String(window.navigator && window.navigator.language || '') : '',
      chromeFullVersion: identityHints && identityHints.chromeFullVersion
    }));
    addCheck('path_prefix', matchesPathPrefixes(window.location && window.location.pathname, conditions.pathPrefixes), bucketPath(window.location && window.location.pathname), conditions.pathPrefixes || 'any');
    if (conditions.initialPathPrefixes) {
      var initialPathname = readInitialDocumentPathname();
      var initialPathMatched = initialPathname !== null && matchesPathPrefixes(initialPathname, conditions.initialPathPrefixes);
      addCheck('initial_path_prefix', initialPathMatched, initialPathname === null ? 'unknown' : initialPathMatched ? 'matched' : 'other', conditions.initialPathPrefixes);
    }
    if (conditions.viewport) {
      addCheck('viewport', inRange(window.innerWidth, conditions.viewport.minWidth, conditions.viewport.maxWidth) && inRange(window.innerHeight, conditions.viewport.minHeight, conditions.viewport.maxHeight), { width: window.innerWidth, height: window.innerHeight }, conditions.viewport);
    }
    if (conditions.screen) {
      addCheck('screen', inRange(window.screen && window.screen.width ? window.screen.width : 0, conditions.screen.minWidth, conditions.screen.maxWidth) && inRange(window.screen && window.screen.height ? window.screen.height : 0, conditions.screen.minHeight, conditions.screen.maxHeight), { width: window.screen && window.screen.width ? window.screen.width : 0, height: window.screen && window.screen.height ? window.screen.height : 0 }, conditions.screen);
    }
    if (conditions.devicePixelRatio) {
      addCheck('device_pixel_ratio', inRange(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, conditions.devicePixelRatio.min, conditions.devicePixelRatio.max), window.devicePixelRatio || 1, conditions.devicePixelRatio);
    }
    if (conditions.referrer && conditions.referrer.absent) {
      addCheck('document_referrer_absent', !document.referrer, bucketReferrer(document.referrer), 'absent');
    }
    if (conditions.requiredLanguagePrefixes) {
      addCheck('navigator_language_prefix', matchesLanguagePrefixes(languages, conditions.requiredLanguagePrefixes), languages.map(function (language) { return truncate(String(language).toLowerCase(), 40); }), conditions.requiredLanguagePrefixes);
    }
    if (conditions.userAgentPattern) {
      addCheck('user_agent_pattern', matchesPattern(userAgent, conditions.userAgentPattern), getBrowserBucket(userAgent) + '/' + getOsBucket(userAgent), conditions.userAgentPattern);
    }
    if (conditions.nonUserAgentPattern) {
      addCheck('non_user_agent_exclusion', !matchesPattern(userAgent, conditions.nonUserAgentPattern), getBrowserBucket(userAgent) + '/' + getOsBucket(userAgent), 'not ' + conditions.nonUserAgentPattern);
    }
    if (Array.isArray(conditions.absentCookieNames)) {
      addCheck('absent_cookies', !conditions.absentCookieNames.some(function (name) { return hasCookie(cookieString, name); }), conditions.absentCookieNames.map(function (name) { return { name: name, present: hasCookie(cookieString, name) }; }), 'absent');
    }

    return {
      conditionChecks: checks,
      // A known mismatch decides the conjunction; missing version evidence alone does not.
      matched: checks.some(function (check) { return check.matched === false; }) ? false
        : checks.some(function (check) { return check.matched === null; }) ? null : true,
    };
  }

  function matchesPathPrefixes(pathname, pathPrefixes) {
    if (!Array.isArray(pathPrefixes)) {
      return true;
    }
    var normalizedPathname = String(pathname || '').replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
    normalizedPathname = normalizedPathname.replace(/^\/(?:en|ko|ja|pl|fr|de|es|es-419|nl|da|sv|no|ru|hu|fi|tr|ro|pt|zh-cn|zh-tw|ar|sr|it|th|vi|cs|el|id)(?=\/|$)/i, '') || '/';
    return pathPrefixes.some(function (pathPrefix) {
      return normalizedPathname === pathPrefix || normalizedPathname.indexOf(pathPrefix + '/') === 0;
    });
  }

  function getClientSignals() {
    var cookieString = document.cookie || '';
    var userAgent = (window.navigator && window.navigator.userAgent) || '';
    return {
      authCookiePresent: authCookieNames.some(function (name) { return hasCookie(cookieString, name); }),
      browserBucket: getBrowserBucket(userAgent),
      devicePixelRatio: window.devicePixelRatio || 1,
      gaCookiePresent: hasCookie(cookieString, '_ga') || hasCookie(cookieString, '_gid') || hasCookiePrefix(cookieString, '_ga_'),
      languages: Array.isArray(window.navigator && window.navigator.languages) ? window.navigator.languages.map(function (language) { return truncate(String(language).toLowerCase(), 40); }) : [],
      osBucket: getOsBucket(userAgent),
      pathBucket: bucketPath(window.location && window.location.pathname),
      referrerBucket: bucketReferrer(document.referrer),
      screen: {
        width: window.screen && window.screen.width ? window.screen.width : 0,
        height: window.screen && window.screen.height ? window.screen.height : 0,
      },
      ua: truncate(userAgent, 300),
      viewport: {
        width: window.innerWidth,
        height: window.innerHeight,
      },
    };
  }

  function sendPayload(payload) {
    var body = JSON.stringify(payload);
    if (navigator.sendBeacon) {
      try {
        if (navigator.sendBeacon(endpoint, new Blob([body], { type: 'application/json' }))) {
          return;
        }
      } catch (error) {}
    }
    if (window.fetch) {
      window.fetch(endpoint, {
        body: body,
        cache: 'no-store',
        credentials: 'same-origin',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        keepalive: true,
        method: 'POST',
      }).catch(function () {});
    }
  }

  function emitProbe(rule) {
    try {
      var initialSuppression = window["__OPGG_AD_SUPPRESS_INITIAL_STATE__"];
      var evaluation = evaluateRule(rule);
      // A later condition mismatch must still report suppression committed before this probe.
      var alreadySuppressed = evaluation.matched === true && rule && window["__OPGG_AD_SUPPRESS_MATCHED_RULE__"] === rule.id && typeof initialSuppression === 'boolean'
        ? initialSuppression
        : Boolean(window[suppressionFlagKey]) || (document.cookie || '').split(';').some(function (cookie) {
          return cookie.trim() === suppressionCookieName + '=' + suppressionCookieValue;
        });
      var clientSignals = getClientSignals();
      var decision = getDecision(evaluation.matched, alreadySuppressed);
      var dedupeKey = [marker, rule.id, decision, clientSignals.pathBucket, clientSignals.screen.width + 'x' + clientSignals.screen.height].join(':');
      try {
        if (window.sessionStorage && window.sessionStorage.getItem(dedupeKey)) {
          return;
        }
        if (window.sessionStorage) {
          window.sessionStorage.setItem(dedupeKey, '1');
        }
      } catch (error) {}

      sendPayload({
        marker: marker,
        source: 'client_gate_probe',
        probeScope: probeScope,
        ruleId: rule.id,
        finalDecision: decision,
        conditionChecks: evaluation.conditionChecks,
        clientSignals: clientSignals,
      });
    } catch (error) {}
  }

  try {
    if (!Array.isArray(rules)) return;
    rules.forEach(function (rule) {
      var hints = window["__OPGG_AD_ANALYTICS_CHROME_HINTS__"];
      // A pending full version is not a final miss; read signals after the loader decision settles.
      if (rule && rule.clientConditions && rule.clientConditions.chromeFullVersion && hints && hints.pending) {
        hints.ready.then(function () { emitProbe(rule); });
      } else {
        emitProbe(rule);
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {}

  function reportDecision(decision) {
    // A path transition before the first decision leaves the landing unobserved.
    if (navigationState.navigatedBeforeDecision || window[residualDecisionKey]) return;
    // The document's inline metadata writer queues the final value before Google configuration.
    try {
      window[residualDecisionKey] = decision;
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event(residualDecisionEvent));
    } catch (error) {}
  }

  function ensureGoogleQueue() {
    window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
    window.gtag = window.gtag || function () { window.dataLayer.push(arguments); };
  }

  function appendScript(id, src, attrs) {
    if (document.getElementById(id)) {
      return;
    }

    var script = document.createElement('script');
    script.id = id;
    script.src = src;
    script.async = true;

    if (attrs) {
      Object.keys(attrs).forEach(function (key) {
        script.setAttribute(key, attrs[key]);
      });
    }

    (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(script);
  }

  function loadGoogleAnalytics() {
    if (window[loadedKey] || window[suppressionFlagKey]) {
      return;
    }

    window[loadedKey] = true;
    ensureGoogleQueue();

    window.dataLayer.push({ 'gtm.start': new Date().getTime(), event: 'gtm.js' });
    appendScript('_next-gtm', 'https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id=' + encodeURIComponent(gtmId), {
      'data-ntpc': 'GTM',
    });

    window.gtag('js', new Date());
    window.gtag('config', gaId);
    appendScript('_next-ga', 'https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=' + encodeURIComponent(gaId));
  }

  function inRange(value, min, max) {
    if (typeof min === 'number' && value < min) {
      return false;

    if (typeof max === 'number' && value > max) {

  function hasCookie(cookieString, name) {

  function matchesLanguagePrefixes(languages, prefixes) {

  function matchesPattern(value, pattern) {

  function matchesPathPrefixes(pathname, pathPrefixes) {
    if (!Array.isArray(pathPrefixes)) {
      return true;
    }

    var normalizedPathname = String(pathname || '').replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
    normalizedPathname = normalizedPathname.replace(
      /^\/(?:en|ko|ja|pl|fr|de|es|es-419|nl|da|sv|no|ru|hu|fi|tr|ro|pt|zh-cn|zh-tw|ar|sr|it|th|vi|cs|el|id)(?=\/|$)/i,
      '',
    ) || '/';

    return pathPrefixes.some(function (pathPrefix) {
      return normalizedPathname === pathPrefix || normalizedPathname.indexOf(pathPrefix + '/') === 0;
    });
  }

  function hasOnlySupportedConditionKeys(conditions) {
    return Object.keys(conditions).every(function (key) {
      return supportedConditionKeys.indexOf(key) >= 0;
    });
  }

  function matchesRule(rule, skipFullVersion) {
    var conditions = rule && rule.clientConditions;
    if (!conditions) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!hasOnlySupportedConditionKeys(conditions)) {
      return false;
    }

    var identityHints = !skipFullVersion && conditions.chromeFullVersion && window["__OPGG_AD_ANALYTICS_CHROME_HINTS__"];
    if (!evaluateAdAnalyticsClientIdentity(conditions, {
      hostname: conditions.hostname && window.location ? window.location.hostname : undefined,
      primaryLanguage: conditions.primaryLanguage ? String(window.navigator && window.navigator.language || '') : '',
      chromeFullVersion: identityHints && identityHints.chromeFullVersion
    }, skipFullVersion).every(function (check) { return check.matched; })) return false;

    if (!matchesPathPrefixes(window.location && window.location.pathname, conditions.pathPrefixes)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (conditions.initialPathPrefixes) {
      var initialPathname = readInitialDocumentPathname();
      if (initialPathname === null || !matchesPathPrefixes(initialPathname, conditions.initialPathPrefixes)) return false;
    }

    if (conditions.viewport) {
      if (
        !inRange(window.innerWidth, conditions.viewport.minWidth, conditions.viewport.maxWidth) ||
        !inRange(window.innerHeight, conditions.viewport.minHeight, conditions.viewport.maxHeight)
      ) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    if (conditions.screen) {
      if (
        !inRange(window.screen && window.screen.width ? window.screen.width : 0, conditions.screen.minWidth, conditions.screen.maxWidth) ||
        !inRange(window.screen && window.screen.height ? window.screen.height : 0, conditions.screen.minHeight, conditions.screen.maxHeight)
      ) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    if (conditions.devicePixelRatio && !inRange(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, conditions.devicePixelRatio.min, conditions.devicePixelRatio.max)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (conditions.referrer && conditions.referrer.absent && document.referrer) {
      return false;
    }

    var languages = [];
    if (Array.isArray(window.navigator && window.navigator.languages)) {
      languages = languages.concat(window.navigator.languages);
    }
    if (window.navigator && window.navigator.language) {
      languages.push(window.navigator.language);
    }
    if (!matchesLanguagePrefixes(languages, conditions.requiredLanguagePrefixes)) {
      return false;
    }

    var userAgent = (window.navigator && window.navigator.userAgent) || '';
    if (conditions.userAgentPattern && !matchesPattern(userAgent, conditions.userAgentPattern)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (conditions.nonUserAgentPattern && matchesPattern(userAgent, conditions.nonUserAgentPattern)) {
      return false;
    }

    var cookieString = document.cookie || '';
    var absentCookieNames = Array.isArray(conditions.absentCookieNames) ? conditions.absentCookieNames : [];
    for (var i = 0; i < absentCookieNames.length; i += 1) {
      if (hasCookie(cookieString, absentCookieNames[i])) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    return true;
  }

  function writeSuppressionCookie(maxAge) {
    var cookieParts = [cookieName + '=' + cookieValue, 'Max-Age=' + maxAge, 'Path=/', 'SameSite=Lax'];
    if (window.location.hostname === 'op.gg' || window.location.hostname.slice(-6) === '.op.gg') {
      cookieParts.push('Domain=' + cookieDomain);
    }
    if (window.location.protocol === 'https:') {
      cookieParts.push('Secure');
    }
    document.cookie = cookieParts.join('; ');
  }

  function commitSuppression(rule) {
    window[suppressionFlagKey] = true;
    window["ga-disable-G-HKZFKE5JEL"]=true;window["ga-disable-G-37HQ1LKWBE"]=true;window["ga-disable-G-WCXGQGETWH"]=true;window["ga-disable-G-HG9DB5ECL8"]=true;
    if (rule) {
      window[matchedRuleKey] = rule.id;
      // Cookie persistence must not undo the current document's suppression decision.
      try { writeSuppressionCookie(rule.rememberForSeconds); } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to persist ad analytics suppression cookie', error);
      }
    }
  }


  var residualDecisionKey = "__OPGG_RESIDUAL_GOOGLE_DECISION__";
  var navigationGateKey = '__OPGG_GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_NAVIGATION_GATE__';
  var navigationState = {
    document: document,
    landingPathname: window.location.pathname,
    pathname: window.location.pathname,
    navigatedBeforeDecision: false,
  };

  function recordNavigation(previousPathname, wasSuppressed) {
    if (previousPathname === navigationState.landingPathname && window.location.pathname !== previousPathname &&
        !window[residualDecisionKey] && !wasSuppressed) {
      navigationState.navigatedBeforeDecision = true;
    }
    navigationState.pathname = window.location.pathname;
    if (navigationState.evaluate) navigationState.evaluate();
  }

  function installNavigationGate(evaluate) {
    var existingState = window[navigationGateKey];
    if (existingState && existingState.document === document) {
      navigationState = existingState;
    } else {
      window[navigationGateKey] = navigationState;
    }
    if (evaluate) navigationState.evaluate = evaluate;
    var history = window.history;
    if (navigationState.installed || !history ||
        typeof history.pushState !== 'function' || typeof history.replaceState !== 'function' ||
        typeof window.addEventListener !== 'function') {
      return;
    }
    navigationState.installed = true;
    // Google wraps these methods later; evaluate after the URL changes but before its wrapper collects.
    ['pushState', 'replaceState'].forEach(function (method) {
      var original = history[method];
      history[method] = function () {
        var previousPathname = window.location.pathname;
        // A destination match must not become evidence of suppression on the landing.
        var wasSuppressed = Boolean(window[suppressionFlagKey]);
        var result = original.apply(this, arguments);
        recordNavigation(previousPathname, wasSuppressed);
        return result;
      };
    });
    window.addEventListener('popstate', function () {
      recordNavigation(navigationState.pathname, Boolean(window[suppressionFlagKey]));
    });
  }


  function applySuppression(rule, reportInitialDecision) {
    commitSuppression(rule);
    // Residual metadata describes the landing decision for this document.
    if (reportInitialDecision) {
      reportDecision('gate_suppressed');
    }
    var matchedRuleId = window[matchedRuleKey];
    try { loadSuppressedReviewAnalytics(typeof matchedRuleId === 'string' ? matchedRuleId : 'client_suppressed'); } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load suppressed review analytics', error);
    }
  }

  function evaluateSuppression(reportInitialDecision) {
    if (window[suppressionFlagKey]) {
      applySuppression(undefined, reportInitialDecision);
      return true;
    }
    var rules = window[clientRulesKey];
    if (!Array.isArray(rules) || rules.length === 0) {
      return false;
    }
    for (var i = 0; i < rules.length; i += 1) {
      if (matchesRule(rules[i])) {
        applySuppression(rules[i], reportInitialDecision);
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  function evaluateNavigationSuppression(reportInitialDecision) {
    if (window[suppressionFlagKey] && reportInitialDecision !== true) {
      return;
    }
    try {
      evaluateSuppression(reportInitialDecision === true);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to evaluate ad analytics suppression', error);
    }
  }

  try { installNavigationGate(evaluateNavigationSuppression); } catch (error) {
    console.error('Failed to install ad analytics navigation gate', error);
  }

  function releaseLoaders() {
    try {
      if (evaluateSuppression(true)) return;
      reportDecision('gate_loaded');
      loadGoogleAnalytics();
    } catch (error) {
      reportDecision('gate_failed_open');
      loadGoogleAnalytics();
    }
  }
  var hints = window["__OPGG_AD_ANALYTICS_CHROME_HINTS__"];
  if (hints && hints.pending) {
    // Capture current synchronous matches before the hint wait can outlive this path.
    evaluateNavigationSuppression(true);
    hints.ready.then(releaseLoaders);
  } else {
    releaseLoaders();
  }
})();
